{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"---\\nlanguage:\\n- en\\nlibrary_name: lerobot\\npipeline_tag: robotics\\ntags:\\n- vision-language-action\\n- imitation-learning\\n- lerobot\\ninference: false\\nlicense: gemma\\n---\\n\\n# π₀ (Pi0) (LeRobot)\\n\\nπ₀ is a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation model from Physical Intelligence that jointly reasons over vision, language, and actions to control robots, serving as the base architecture that later enabled π₀.₅’s open-world generalization.\\n\\n\\n**Original paper:** π0: A Vision-Language-Action Flow Model for General Robot Controlion  \\n**Reference implementation:** https://github.com/Physical-Intelligence/openpi  \\n**LeRobot implementation:** Follows the original reference code for compatibility.\\n\\n\\n## Model description\\n\\n- **Inputs:** images (multi-view), proprio/state, optional language instruction\\n- **Outputs:** continuous actions\\n- **Training objective:** flow matching\\n- **Action representation:** continuous\\n- **Intended use:** Base model to fine tune on your specific use case\\n\\n\\n## Quick start (inference on a real batch)\\n\\n### Installation\\n\\n```bash\\npip install \\\"lerobot[pi]@git+https://github.com/huggingface/lerobot.git\\\"\\n```\\nFor full installation details (including optional video dependencies such as ffmpeg for torchcodec), see the official documentation: https://huggingface.co/docs/lerobot/installation\\n\\n### Load model + dataset, run `select_action`\\n\\n```python\\nimport torch\\nfrom lerobot.datasets.lerobot_dataset import LeRobotDataset\\nfrom lerobot.policies.factory import make_pre_post_processors\\n\\n# Swap this import per-policy\\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi0 import PI0Policy\\n\\n# load a policy\\nmodel_id = \\\"lerobot/pi0_libero_base\\\"  # <- swap checkpoint\\ndevice = torch.device(\\\"cuda\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\"cpu\\\")\\n\\npolicy = PI0Policy.from_pretrained(model_id).to(device).eval()\\n\\npreprocess, postprocess = make_pre_post_processors(\\n    policy.config,\\n    model_id,\\n    preprocessor_overrides={\\\"device_processor\\\": {\\\"device\\\": str(device)}},\\n)\\n# load a lerobotdataset\\ndataset = LeRobotDataset(\\\"lerobot/libero\\\")\\n\\n# pick an episode\\nepisode_index = 0\\n\\n# each episode corresponds to a contiguous range of frame indices\\nfrom_idx = dataset.meta.episodes[\\\"dataset_from_index\\\"][episode_index]\\nto_idx   = dataset.meta.episodes[\\\"dataset_to_index\\\"][episode_index]\\n\\n# get a single frame from that episode (e.g. the first frame)\\nframe_index = from_idx\\nframe = dict(dataset[frame_index])\\n\\nbatch = preprocess(frame)\\nwith torch.inference_mode():\\n    pred_action = policy.select_action(frame)\\n    # use your policy postprocess, this post process the action\\n    # for instance unnormalize the actions, detokenize it etc..\\n    pred_action = postprocess(pred_action)\\n```\\n\\n\\n## Training step (loss + backward)\\n\\nIf you’re training / fine-tuning, you typically call `forward(...)` to get a loss and then:\\n\\n```python\\npolicy.train()\\nbatch = dict(dataset[0])\\nbatch = preprocess(batch)\\n\\nloss, outputs = policy.forward(batch)\\nloss.backward()\\n\\n```\\n\\n> Notes:\\n> \\n> - Some policies expose `policy(**batch)` or return a dict; keep this snippet aligned with the policy API.\\n> - Use your trainer script (`lerobot-train`) for full training loops.\\n\\n\\n## How to train / fine-tune\\n\\n```bash\\nlerobot-train \\\\\\n  --dataset.repo_id=${HF_USER}/&lt;dataset> \\\\\\n  --output_dir=./outputs/[RUN_NAME] \\\\\\n  --job_name=[RUN_NAME] \\\\\\n  --policy.repo_id=${HF_USER}/&lt;desired_policy_repo_id> \\\\\\n  --policy.path=lerobot/[BASE_CHECKPOINT] \\\\\\n  --policy.dtype=bfloat16 \\\\\\n  --policy.device=cuda \\\\\\n  --steps=100000 \\\\\\n  --batch_size=4\\n```\\n\\nAdd policy-specific flags below:\\n\\n- `-policy.chunk_size=...`\\n- `-policy.n_action_steps=...`\\n- `-policy.max_action_tokens=...`\\n- `-policy.gradient_checkpointing=true`\\n\\n\\n## Evaluate in Simulation (LIBERO)\\n\\nYou can evaluate the model in Libero environment.\\n\\n```bash\\nlerobot-eval \\\\\\n  --policy.path=lerobot/pi0_libero_base \\\\\\n  --env.type=libero \\\\\\n  --env.task=libero_object \\\\\\n  --eval.batch_size=1 \\\\\\n  --eval.n_episodes=20\\n```\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.5}}"}

Options: libero_spatial, libero_object, libero_goal, libero_10, libero_90\\n    )\\n    num_steps_wait: int = 10  # Number of steps to wait for objects to stabilize i n sim\\n    num_trials_per_task: int = 50  # Number of rollouts per task\\n\\n    #################################################################################################################\\n    # Utils\\n    #################################################################################################################\\n    video_out_path: str = \\\"data/libero/videos\\\"  # Path to save videos\\n\\n    seed: int = 7  # Random Seed (for reproducibility)\\n\\n\\ndef eval_libero(args: Args) -> None:\\n    # Set random seed\\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\\n\\n    # Initialize LIBERO task suite\\n    benchmark_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\\n    task_suite = benchmark_dict[args.task_suite_name]()\\n    num_tasks_in_suite = task_suite.n_tasks\\n    logging.info(f\\\"Task suite: {args.task_suite_name}\\\")\\n\\n    pathlib.Path(args.video_out_path).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    if args.task_suite_name == \\\"libero_spatial\\\":\\n        max_steps = 220  # longest training demo has 193 steps\\n    elif args.task_suite_name == \\\"libero_object\\\":\\n        max_steps = 280  # longest training demo has 254 steps\\n    elif args.task_suite_name == \\\"libero_goal\\\":\\n        max_steps = 300  # longest training demo has 270 steps\\n    elif args.task_suite_name == \\\"libero_10\\\":\\n        max_steps = 520  # longest training demo has 505 steps\\n    elif args.task_suite_name == \\\"libero_90\\\":\\n        max_steps = 400  # longest training demo has 373 steps\\n    else:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"Unknown task suite: {args.task_suite_name}\\\")\\n\\n    client = _websocket_client_policy.WebsocketClientPolicy(args.host, args.port)\\n\\n    # Start evaluation\\n    total_episodes, total_successes = 0, 0\\n    for task_id in tqdm.tqdm(range(num_tasks_in_suite)):\\n        # Get task\\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\\n\\n        # Get default LIBERO initial states\\n        initial_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\\n\\n        # Initialize LIBERO environment and task description\\n        env, task_description = _get_libero_env(task, LIBERO_ENV_RESOLUTION, args.seed)\\n\\n        # Start episodes\\n        task_episodes, task_successes = 0, 0\\n        for episode_idx in tqdm.tqdm(range(args.num_trials_per_task)):\\n            logging.info(f\\\"\\\\nTask: {task_description}\\\")\\n\\n            # Reset environment\\n            env.reset()\\n            action_plan = collections.deque()\\n\\n            # Set initial states\\n            obs = env.set_init_state(initial_states[episode_idx])\\n\\n            # Setup\\n            t = 0\\n            replay_images = []\\n\\n            logging.info(f\\\"Starting episode {task_episodes+1}...\\\")\\n            while t < max\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 1194 字符] ...\\n\\n         if not action_plan:\\n                        # Finished executing previous action chunk -- compute new chunk\\n                        # Prepare observations dict\\n                        element = {\\n                            \\\"observation/image\\\": img,\\n                            \\\"observation/wrist_image\\\": wrist_img,\\n                            \\\"observation/state\\\": np.concatenate(\\n                                (\\n                                    obs[\\\"robot0_eef_pos\\\"],\\n                                    _quat2axisangle(obs[\\\"robot0_eef_quat\\\"]),\\n                                    obs[\\\"robot0_gripper_qpos\\\"],\\n                                )\\n                            ),\\n                            \\\"prompt\\\": str(task_description),\\n                        }\\n\\n                        # Query model to get action\\n                        action_chunk = client.infer(element)[\\\"actions\\\"]\\n                        assert (\\n                            len(action_chunk) >= args.replan_steps\\n                        ), f\\\"We want to replan every {args.replan_steps} steps, but policy only predicts {len(action_chunk)} steps.\\\"\\n                        action_plan.extend(action_chunk[: args.replan_steps])\\n\\n                    action = action_plan.popleft()\\n\\n                    # Execute action in environment\\n                    obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\\n                    if done:\\n                        task_successes += 1\\n                        total_successes += 1\\n                        break\\n                    t += 1\\n\\n                except Exception as e:\\n                    logging.error(f\\\"Caught exception: {e}\\\")\\n                    break\\n\\n            task_episodes += 1\\n            total_episodes += 1\\n\\n            # Save a replay video of the episode\\n            suffix = \\\"success\\\" if done else \\\"failure\\\"\\n            task_segment = task_description.replace(\\\" \\\", \\\"_\\\")\\n            imageio.mimwrite(\\n                pathlib.Path(args.video_out_path) / f\\\"rollout_{task_segment}_{suffix}.mp4\\\",\\n                [np.asarray(x) for x in replay_images],\\n                fps=10,\\n            )\\n\\n            # Log current results\\n            logging.info(f\\\"Success: {done}\\\")\\n            logging.info(f\\\"# episodes completed so far: {total_episodes}\\\")\\n            logging.info(f\\\"# successes: {total_successes} ({total_successes / total_episodes * 100:.1f}%)\\\")\\n\\n        # Log final results\\n        logging.info(f\\\"Current task success rate: {float(task_successes) / float(task_episodes)}\\\")\\n        logging.info(f\\\"Current total success rate: {float(total_successes) / float(total_episodes)}\\\")\\n\\n    logging.info(f\\\"Total success rate: {float(total_successes) / float(total_episodes)}\\\")\\n    logging.info(f\\\"Total episodes: {total_episodes}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef _get_libero_env(task, resolution, seed):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Initializes and returns the LIBERO environment, along with the task description.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    task_description = task.language\\n    task_bddl_file = pathlib.Path(get_libero_path(\\\"bddl_files\\\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\\n    env_args = {\\\"bddl_file_name\\\": task_bddl_file, \\\"camera_heights\\\": resolution, \\\"camera_widths\\\": resolution}\\n    env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\\n    env.seed(seed)  # IMPORTANT: seed seems to affect object positions even when using fixed initial state\\n    return env, task_description\\n\\n\\ndef _quat2axisangle(quat):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Copied from robosuite: https://github.com/ARISE-Initiative/robosuite/blob/eafb81f54ffc104f905ee48a16bb15f059176ad3/robosuite/utils/transform_utils.py#L490C1-L512C55\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # clip quaternion\\n    if quat[3] > 1.0:\\n        quat[3] = 1.0\\n    elif quat[3] < -1.0:\\n        quat[3] = -1.0\\n\\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - quat[3] * quat[3])\\n    if math.isclose(den, 0.0):\\n        # This is (close to) a zero degree rotation, immediately return\\n        return np.zeros(3)\\n\\n    return (quat[:3] * 2.0 * math.acos(quat[3])) / den\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)\\n    tyro.cli(eval_libero)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}

### Assistant

_source line 26_

重要发现：openpi 的 `examples/libero/main.py` 默认 task suite 是 `libero_spatial`，但这是一个**客户端-服务器模式**的 eval（需要先启动模型服务器）。现在看看我们的 evaluate_libero_plus.py 用的 task suite，以及 lerobot 的 eval 方式。

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"import dataclasses\\n\\nimport einops\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nfrom openpi import transforms\\nfrom openpi.models import model as _model\\n\\n\\ndef make_libero_example() -> dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Creates a random input example for the Libero policy.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return {\\n        \\\"observation/state\\\": np.random.rand(8),\\n        \\\"observation/image\\\": np.random.randint(256, size=(224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\\n        \\\"observation/wrist_image\\\": np.random.randint(256, size=(224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\\n        \\\"prompt\\\": \\\"do something\\\",\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef _parse_image(image) -> np.ndarray:\\n    image = np.asarray(image)\\n    if np.issubdtype(image.dtype, np.floating):\\n        image = (255 * image).astype(np.uint8)\\n    if image.shape[0] == 3:\\n        image = einops.rearrange(image, \\\"c h w -> h w c\\\")\\n    return image\\n\\n\\n@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True)\\nclass LiberoInputs(transforms.DataTransformFn):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    This class is used to convert inputs to the model to the expected format. If your dataset does not have a particular type\\n        # of image, e.g. wrist images, you can comment it out here and replace it with zeros like we do for the\\n        # right wrist image below.\\n        base_image = _parse_image(data[\\\"observation/image\\\"])\\n        wrist_image = _parse_image(data[\\\"observation/wrist_image\\\"])\\n\\n        # Create inputs dict. Do not change the keys in the dict below.\\n        inputs = {\\n            \\\"state\\\": data[\\\"observation/state\\\"],\\n            \\\"image\\\": {\\n                \\\"base_0_rgb\\\": base_image,\\n                \\\"left_wrist_0_rgb\\\": wrist_image,\\n                # Pad any non-existent images with zero-arrays of the appropriate shape.\\n                \\\"right_wrist_0_rgb\\\": np.zeros_like(base_image),\\n            },\\n            \\\"image_mask\\\": {\\n                \\\"base_0_rgb\\\": np.True_,\\n                \\\"left_wrist_0_rgb\\\": np.True_,\\n                # We only mask padding images for pi0 model, not pi0-FAST. ### Assistant

_source line 56_

关键发现！我们的 `evaluate_libero_plus.py` 默认用 `libero_spatial`（第469行），而 pi0_libero checkpoint README 明确说用 `--env.task=libero_object` 做 eval。

但注意：README 里的模型名是 `lerobot/pi0_libero_base`，我们需要确认这个 checkpoint 到底是 base 还是某个特定 task suite fine-tuned 的版本。让我进一步确认，并尝试用 lerobot-eval 原生方式跑。

### Assistant

_source line 112_

进程还活着但日志不变——可能在初始化 2500+ 个 MuJoCo 环境。这个方法创建了太多环境，效率很低。让我 kill 它，改成用 `--eval.n_episodes=1` 并且看看能否限制 task_ids。

不过更重要的是：2518 个 task orders 意味着这个 LIBERO-Plus 安装把 libero_object 包含了大量 task variants。这可能不是标准 LIBERO benchmark（标准 libero_object 只有 10 个 task）。

让我先 kill 这个进程，然后用 openpi 的 `examples/libero/main.py` 方式做一个更简单的单 task 验证。这个脚本使用 websocket client-server 模式，但我可以直接写一个不需要 server 的最小验证脚本。

  "command": "cat > /tmp/minimal_eval.py << 'EOF'\n\"\"\"Minimal eval: pi0_libero on 1 task from libero_object, using LeRobot native.\"\"\"\nimport torch\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Load policy\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi0 import PI0Policy\nfrom lerobot.policies.factory import make_pre_post_processors\n\nmodel_path = \"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero\"\ndevice = torch.device(\"cuda\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n\nprint(f\"Loading policy from {model_path}...\")\npolicy = PI0Policy.from_pretrained(model_path).to(device).eval()\npreprocess, postprocess = make_pre_post_processors(\n    policy.config,\n    model_path,\n    preprocessor_overrides={\"device_processor\": {\"device\": str(device)}},\n)\nprint(f\"Policy loaded. device={device}\")\n\n# Setup LIBERO env (single task from libero_object)\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark, get_libero_path\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\ntask_suite_name = \"libero_object\"\nbenchmark_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\ntask_suite = benchmark_dict[task_suite_name]()\nnum_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\nprint(f\"Task suite: {task_suite_name}, num_tasks={num_tasks}\")\n\n# Use first task\ntask_id = 0\ntask = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\ntask_description = task.language\nprint(f\"Task {task_id}: {task_description}\")\n\n# Create env\nbddl_file = Path(get_libero_path(\"bddl_files\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\nenv_args = {\"bddl_file_name\": str(bddl_file), \"camera_heights\": 256, \"camera_widths\": 256}\nenv = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\nenv.seed(42)\n\n# Get initial states\ninitial_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n\n# Run 3 episodes\nmax_steps = 280\nn_episodes = 3\nsuccesses = 0\n\nfor ep in range(n_episodes):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(initial_states[ep])\n    \n    # Wait for objects to settle\n    for _ in range(10):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step([0.0]*6 + [-1.0])\n    \n    done = False\n    for t in range(max_steps):\n        # Get images\n        img = obs[\"agentview_image\"]  # (256,256,3) uint8\n        wrist_img = obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]  # (256,256,3) uint8\n        \n        # Get state\n        state = np.concatenate([\n            obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"],\n            obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"],  # 4D quaternion -> but config says 8D state\n            obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"],\n        ])\n        \n        # Build frame dict matching LeRobot format\n        frame = {\n            \"observation.images.image\": torch.from_numpy(img).permute(2,0,1).float() / 255.0,\n            \"observation.images.image2\": torch.from_numpy(wrist_img).permute(2,0,1).float() / 255.0,\n            \"observation.state\": torch.from_numpy(state).float(),\n            \"task\": task_description,\n        }\n        \n        batch = preprocess(frame)\n        with torch.inference_mode():\n            action = policy.select_action(batch)\n            action = postprocess(action)\n        \n        # action should be dict or tensor\n        if isinstance(action, dict):\n            act = action[\"action\"].cpu().numpy() if \"action\" in action else list(action.values())[0].cpu().numpy()\n        elif isinstance(action, torch.Tensor):\n            act = action.cpu().numpy()\n        else:\n            act = np.array(action)\n        \n        # Take first action step (action might be chunked)\n        if act.ndim == 2:\n            act = act[0]\n        act = act[:7]  # Only first 7 dims\n        \n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(act.tolist())\n        if done:\n            successes += 1\n            print(f\"  Episode {ep}: SUCCESS at step {t}\")\n            break\n    \n    if not done:\n        print(f\"  Episode {ep}: FAILED (max_steps reached)\")\n\nprint(f\"\\nResult: {successes}/{n_episodes} success rate = {successes/n_episodes*100:.1f}%\")\nenv.close()\nEOF\necho \"Script written\"",
  "account": "root"
}
```

### Assistant

_source line 194_

checkpoint 里没有 tokenizer 文件。LeRobot 代码中 PI0Policy 在推理时需要 PaLI-Gemma tokenizer 来处理 language prompt。需要提供本地 tokenizer。

让我看看 lerobot 的 PI0Policy 是从哪获取 tokenizer 的，以及是否有 gemma tokenizer 已缓存在系统中。

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"            output_transforms=[\\n                *data_config.model_transforms.outputs,\\n                transforms.Unnormalize(norm_stats, use_quantiles=data_config.use_quantile_norm),\\n                *data_config.data_transforms.outputs,\\n            ],\\n            sample_kwargs=None,\\n            metadata=train_config.policy_metadata,\\n            is_pytorch=True,\\n            pytorch_device=device,\\n        )\\n        log.info(\\\"OpenPI Policy built successfully\\\")\\n\\n        return cls(policy, model, routing_mode=routing_mode, device=device)\\n\\n    def infer(self, obs_dict: dict) -> dict:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Run inference using the openpi Policy.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            obs_dict: Dict with keys matching LIBERO format:\\n                - \\\"observation/image\\\": uint8 (224,224,3)\\n                - \\\"observation/wrist_image\\\": uint8 (224,224,3)\\n                - \\\"observation/state\\\": float32 (8,)\\n                - \\\"prompt\\\": str\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Dict with \\\"actions\\\" (numpy array, shape (horizon, 7))\\n            and optionally \\\"routing_weights\\\".\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        result = self.policy.infer(obs_dict)\\n\\n        output = {\\\"actions\\\": result[\\\"actions\\\"]}\\n\\n        if self.routing_mode == 'none':\\n            output[\\\"routing_weights\\\"] = np.array([0.0, 0.0, 0.0])\\n        else:\\n            output[\\\"routing_weights\\\"] = np.array([0.33, 0.33, 0.34])\\n\\n        return output\\n\\n    def set_oracle_label(self, perturbation_type: str):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"No-op for routing_mode='none'. inference.\nTests on BOTH libero_object and libero_spatial to determine task suite match.\n\"\"\"\nimport sys\nsys.path.insert(0, '/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1')\nsys.path.insert(0, '/root/meta_brain_lora_routing')\n\nimport collections\nimport logging\nimport math\nimport numpy as np\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport os\n\nos.environ['CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES'] = '1'\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\")\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# Load policy\nfrom openpi_metabrain_policy import OpenPIMetaBrainPolicy\n\nlog.info(\"Loading OpenPI policy...\")\npolicy = OpenPIMetaBrainPolicy.from_checkpoint(\n    checkpoint_path='/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero',\n    routing_mode='none',\n    device='cuda:0',\n)\nlog.info(\"Policy loaded!\")\n\n# Setup LIBERO env\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark, get_libero_path\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\ndef quat2axisangle(quat):\n    if quat[3] > 1.0: quat[3] = 1.0\n    elif quat[3] < -1.0: quat[3] = -1.0\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - quat[3] * quat[3])\n    if math.isclose(den, 0.0):\n        return np.zeros(3)\n    return (quat[:3] * 2.0 * math.acos(quat[3])) / den\n\ndef eval_suite(suite_name, num_tasks=2, episodes_per_task=3, max_steps=300):\n    \"\"\"Evaluate on a task suite, return success rate.\"\"\"\n    log.info(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n    log.info(f\"Evaluating on: {suite_name}\")\n    log.info(f\"{'='*60}\")\n    \n    benchmark_dict = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()\n    task_suite = benchmark_dict[suite_name]()\n    total_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n    log.info(f\"Suite has {total_tasks} tasks, evaluating {num_tasks}\")\n    \n    total_episodes = 0\n    total_successes = 0\n    \n    for task_id in range(min(num_tasks, total_tasks)):\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_description = task.language\n        log.info(f\"\\n  Task {task_id}: {task_description}\")\n        \n        # Create env\n        bddl_file = Path(get_libero_path(\"bddl_files\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\n        env_args = {\"bddl_file_name\": str(bddl_file), \"camera_heights\": 256, \"camera_widths\": 256}\n        env = OffScreenRenderEnv(**env_args)\n        env.seed(42)\n        \n        initial_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(task_id)\n        task_successes = 0\n        \n        for ep in range(episodes_per_task):\n            env.reset()\n            obs = env.set_init_state(initial_states[ep])\n            \n            # Wait for objects to settle\n            for _ in range(10):\n                obs, _, _, _ = env.step([0.0]*6 + [-1.0])\n            \n            done = False\n            action_plan = collections.deque()\n            replan_steps = 5\n            \n            for t in range(max_steps):\n                if not action_plan:\n                    # Get observation\n                    img = obs[\"agentview_image\"]  # (256, 256, 3) uint8\n                    wrist_img = obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]  # (256, 256, 3) uint8\n                    \n                    # Build state: pos(3) + axisangle(3) + gripper(2) = 8\n                    state = np.concatenate([\n                        obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"],\n                        quat2axisangle(obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"].copy()),\n                        obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"],\n                    ]).astype(np.float32)\n                    \n                    # Build obs dict for openpi\n                    obs_dict = {\n                        \"observation/image\": img,\n                        \"observation/wrist_image\": wrist_img,\n                        \"observation/state\": state,\n                        \"prompt\": str(task_description),\n                    }\n                    \n                    # Infer\n                    result = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                    action_chunk = result[\"actions\"]\n                    \n                    if t == 0 and ep == 0:\n                        log.info(f\"    Action chunk shape: {action_chunk.shape}\")\n                        log.info(f\"    Action[0]: {action_chunk[0]}\")\n                        log.info(f\"    Action range: [{action_chunk.min():.4f}, {action_chunk.max():.4f}]\")\n                    \n                    # Use first replan_steps actions\n                    for i in range(min(replan_steps, len(action_chunk))):\n                        action_plan.append(action_chunk[i])\n                \n                action = action_plan.popleft()\n                obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n                \n                if done:\n                    task_successes += 1\n                    total_successes += 1\n                    log.info(f\"    Episode {ep}: SUCCESS at step {t}\")\n                    break\n            \n            if not done:\n                log.info(f\"    Episode {ep}: FAILED (max_steps)\")\n            total_episodes += 1\n        \n        env.close()\n        log.info(f\"  Task {task_id} result: {task_successes}/{episodes_per_task}\")\n    \n    rate = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0\n    log.info(f\"\\n  {suite_name} TOTAL: {total_successes}/{total_episodes} = {rate*100:.1f}%\")\n    return rate\n\n# Evaluate both suites\nresults = {}\nresults[\"libero_object\"] = eval_suite(\"libero_object\", num_tasks=3, episodes_per_task=3, max_steps=280)\nresults[\"libero_spatial\"] = eval_suite(\"libero_spatial\", num_tasks=3, episodes_per_task=3, max_steps=220)\n\nlog.info(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\nlog.info(\"FINAL RESULTS:\")\nfor suite, rate in results.items():\n    log.info(f\"  {suite}: {rate*100:.1f}%\")\nlog.info(\"=\"*60)\n\npolicy.cleanup()\nSCRIPT\necho \"Script written\"",
